HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_06a7f08d6244d2f7e6b6","text":"4. **Trusting assent after experienced safety.** Lexical evidence: ء م ن B002 (trusting assent and confirmation), ء م ن B001 (settled security), ء ل ه B001/ع ب د B003 from 106:0/3 (worshipped one; worship), and س م و B005 from 106:0 (raised designation/name). Structural cue: the sequence names God, narrates habituation, commands worship, then states experienced security. Assigned role: ء م ن B002 lets the safety claim become a basis for assent: the protected people can confirm the named Lord by their worship. Abductive move: I infer that bodily/social security grounds a movement from safety to acknowledgment. Reading change: \"ءَامَنَهُم\" can remain \"made them secure\" while also activating the logic by which security calls forth trusting recognition.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000934/B004","root_000934/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1408ae13ce281b45c544","text":"2. **Feeding expands into durable livelihood assembled through travel.** Fixed `أَطْعَمَهُم` activates `ط ع م B004` (provision, livelihood, earnings, revenue, and good condition), while fixed `جُوع` activates `ج و ع B002` (famine-time). The earlier habitual seasonal journey and compositional `إِۦلَٰف` (`ء ل ف B002`) supply a recurring distribution network rather than a single meal. As a material model, `ط ع م B010` (a graft that accepts an inserted branch) makes the external journey legs into provision-bearing connections taken into the local house. The packet supplies livelihood, joining, grafting, journey, and famine branches; I infer goods moving through that network and being incorporated locally. Feeding therefore reads as maintaining a resilient economy, with the graft image exploratory, not merely as handing over food once.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_14fdfd8177bf1cb57b09","text":"No later ayah follows 106:4, so there was no subsequent contextual surprise in the strict forward sense. The retrospective change came from rereading the whole packet into this ending: earlier \"إِيلَاف\" and \"رِحْلَة\" prevent \"أَطْعَمَهُم\" from shrinking to isolated food, and earlier \"رَبَّ هَٰذَا ٱلْبَيْتِ\" prevents \"ءَامَنَهُم\" from shrinking to private calm. The final ayah gathers the preceding mechanisms: habituated attachment, seasonal movement, house-centered worship, nurture, livelihood, and safe-conduct all become visible as the concrete content of the Lord's action.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_26b7cafe268f4b6064ad","text":"2. **Security as safe-conduct for travel.** Lexical evidence: ء م ن B001 (safe-conduct, place of safety, trust), خ و ف B001/B002 (fear and fear-causing intimidation), ر ح ل B001/B007/B009 in 106:2 (journey; forced removal; harmful burden), and ب ي ت B001 in 106:3 (shelter). Structural cue: \"ءَامَنَهُم مِّنْ خَوْفٍ\" answers the exposed mobility of \"رِحْلَةَ\". Assigned role: security converts dangerous movement into trusted passage. Abductive move: I infer that fear here includes the route's social violence and threat of dislodgement, not only inner emotion. Reading change: the ayah becomes an account of protected circulation: they are made able to move without being turned into victims of the road.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000447/B004","root_000447/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3289ff97634d8e3eefc2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"106:4:7\",\"root_000447\",[\"B004\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Korku, beklenen zararın yanı sıra malı, insanı ve hareket kapasitesini eksilten, hayatı görünür biçimde daraltan bir durum olarak da okunabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000776/B003","root_000934/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_36bb7a1265cd94fde142","text":"3. **Feeding can occur through ecological maturation.** Fixed `أَطْعَمَهُم` also activates `ط ع م B005` (fruit or plants ripening and taking flavor). The earlier fixed seasons carry `ش ت و B003` (winter rain) and `ص ي ف B002` (summer rain, vegetation, and pasture), while the preceding `رَبّ` activates `ر ب ب B002` (nurture to completion). Ripening is the mediating role between seasonal ecology and edible provision. I infer the arrow seasonal water/conditions -> maturation -> feeding; the packet explicitly supplies vegetation only in the summer-rain branch. The causative feeding consequently includes bringing sources of food to maturity, not solely delivering finished food.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000934/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_43c9dcd0284687ee1cb8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"106:4:2\",\"root_000934\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Besleme, mevsimsel koşulların ürünü olgunlaştırması yoluyla gıdayı hazır hale getiren bir süreç boyutu da kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000447/B001","root_000934/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_598b127cf75ad680b14b","text":"1. **Two coordinated exits from deprivation.** Fixed `أَطْعَمَهُم` activates `ط ع م B002` (feeding another), fixed `جُوع` activates `ج و ع B001` (the empty stomach and hunger-pang), fixed `ءَامَنَهُم` activates `ء م ن B001` (settled security and trust), and fixed `خَوْف` activates `خ و ف B001` (anticipation of harm). The exact parallel `verb + هم + مِن + threat`, repeated on both sides of `وَ`, pairs bodily emptiness with anticipatory disturbance. Feeding fills the first lack; securing settles the second. The packet supplies the two causatives and their objects; I infer that the repeated `مِن` presents both threats as states out of which the group is transferred. The ayah reads as a coordinated material and affective release rather than two unrelated favors.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6c6691a043fa7e2930f4","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"106:4:5\",\"root_000054\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Güvenlik, iç huzurun yanında, güzergâhları, taşıyıcıları ve ilişkileri tekrarlanan yolculuk için güvenilir kılan emniyetli geçişi de kapsar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B001","root_000447/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_77012c69d543da296df8","text":"4. **Security includes trusted passage and reliable relations.** Fixed `ءَامَنَهُم` activates the safe-conduct, trust, entrusted-object, and reliable-mount scope of `ء م ن B001`, not only its opposition to fear. Its position after the seasonal journey and familiar attachment (`ء ل ف B005`) makes reliability across repeated movement the structural mechanism; fixed `خَوْف` with `خ و ف B001` supplies anticipated interruption. Security's assigned role is to make routes, carriers, and relations dependable enough for recurrence. The packet names only the divine securing, so I infer these social and logistical realizations rather than attributing them explicitly. The clause thus reads as production of a trustworthy field of movement as well as inward calm.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000934/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_77b99ae6c65afcf398bc","text":"1. **Feeding as more than removing an empty stomach.** Lexical evidence: ط ع م B002 (feeding another), B004 (provision, livelihood, good condition), ج و ع B001 (empty stomach), and ج و ع B002 (famine-time). Structural cue: the ayah pairs a positive act \"أَطْعَمَهُم\" with \"مِّن جُوعٍ\" after 106:2's winter/summer cycle. Assigned role: feeding fills immediate hunger while B004 extends it to livelihood and social good condition. Abductive move: I infer that the divine act covers both acute hunger and the economy that prevents famine. Reading change: the fixed ayah reads as provisioned subsistence, not only a single meal.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000278/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7cc117aa539dd3a51abd","text":"3. **Famine-season and seasonal rain behind hunger.** Lexical evidence: ج و ع B002 (famine-time), ش ت و B004 in 106:2 (winter as famine/privation), ش ت و B003 (winter rain), ص ي ف B002 (summer rain), ط ع م B005 (ripening produce), and ر ب ب B008/B012/B013 in 106:3 (cloud nurturing plants; green vegetation; abundant water). Structural cue: the final hunger clause retrospectively lights up the seasonal pair. Assigned role: the seasonal/rain branches provide the ecological mechanism by which hunger is prevented. Abductive move: I infer that feeding includes climate-timed provision through rain, crops, and travel-enabled access to food. Reading change: \"جوع\" reads as an environmental-economic vulnerability spanning bad seasons, not merely individual appetite.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8448ce55847603a8ff1a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"106:4:5\",\"root_000054\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Yaşanan güvenliğin kulluğa götüren güven ve onayı doğurduğu da düşünülebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000278/B002","root_000934/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_bca14450682bc5966aa5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"106:4:2\",\"root_000934\",[\"B004\"]],[\"106:4:4\",\"root_000278\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Besleme, tek seferlik doyurmanın ötesinde, kıtlık dönemlerine karşı geçim kaynaklarını ve toplumsal iyi durumu sürdüren kalıcı bir düzeni kapsar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c970d53ab33608c33d84","text":"No later ayah exists within the clipped surah window, so no later-ayah surprise or correction emerged. The retained activations come from the earlier circuit and house plus the fixed ayah's own exact parallelism.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000934/B010"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_cf55da0638a61b4e07ac","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"106:4:2\",\"root_000934\",[\"B010\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Besleme, yolculuk kollarından gelen kaynakların yerel düzene aşılanması biçiminde de tasarlanabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000278/B004","root_000447/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d4c63cdfca08fee9f9de","text":"5. **Relief from fear as relief from visible diminution.** Lexical evidence: خ و ف B004/B005 (diminishing by taking away; visible manifestation of fear), ج و ع B004 (hunger as emptiness/leanness), and ط ع م B004/B007 (good condition; animal fatness). Structural cue: hunger and fear are paired as two reductions: one empties the body/economy, the other visibly contracts or diminishes life. Assigned role: the non-primary fear branches make security restorative, returning fullness, motion, and public composure. Abductive move: I infer that fear is not only a feeling but a social state that visibly reduces capacity. Reading change: the final ayah reads as rescue from depletion: fed from emptiness and secured from shrinkage/intimidation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000447/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_de1a8cdbd4a19c66580b","text":"5. **Fear is also erosive loss.** Fixed `خَوْف` normally activates `خ و ف B001`, but the parallel with emptying hunger activates dormant `خ و ف B004` (diminishing by taking away). The two halves then share a subtraction structure: hunger depletes the body, while danger can deplete goods, people, or capacity; feeding replenishes one and security blocks the other. Only bodily emptiness and fear are explicit; fear -> material or social attrition is the abductive arrow. `مِّنْ خَوْفٍ` therefore admits rescue from ongoing erosion in addition to rescue from the expectation of harm.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
